Only `ArtifactKind` (13 values) has 10+ values.\n\n---\n\n## Type system issues (deep review findings, 2026-05-12)\n\n### The `graph: Any` gap — single most consequential issue\n\nCentral graph domain object passed as `Any` everywhere (`qc/store.py:215`, `conditional_edge_quality.py:240`, dozens more). Every `getattr(graph, \"run_id\", \"\")` is untyped dispatch. A `GraphLike(Protocol)` eliminates dozens of `getattr` calls and enables checker enforcement.\n\n### `dict[str, Any]` on domain dataclass fields\n\nFields with known shapes typed as `Any` (`qc/store.py`: `input_json`, `axes`, `index_json`; `contracts/questions.py`: `data` on `QuestionRead`; `tiers.py` cache returns). Use `TypedDict` or Pydantic models.\n\n### Import-time filesystem side effects\n\n`SYSTEM_PROMPT = _load_system_prompt()` at module level in all three Fermi files. Importing the module unconditionally hits the filesystem. Lazy-load with `@lru_cache` instead.\n\n### `_ensure_database()` called inside read functions\n\n`qc/store.py` calls `_ensure_database()` (which runs `create_all()`) inside every read. DDL on read paths is wrong. Schema init belongs at startup.\n\n### `assert` as a runtime guard\n\n`assert stored is not None` in `qc/store.py:152`. Disabled with `-O`. Use `raise RuntimeError(...)` or a typed result.\n\n### `except Exception` with swallowed errors\n\n`conditional_edge_quality.py`: bare `except Exception` converting real errors to dict entries, marked `# pragma: no cover`. Silent failure path.\n\n### Private symbol imports across modules\n\n`from packages.storage.graph_store import _edges_payload, _write_json` — couples to internal implementation.\n\n### Truthiness bug in Fermi files\n\n`f\"{horizon_days} days\" if horizon_days else (horizon_bucket or \"unknown\")` — if `horizon_days = 0`, falsy branch wrongly fires. Use `is not None`.\n\n### `str` where `Literal`/`StrEnum` belongs — contracts\n\n* `target_type: str  # \"binary\" | \"categorical\" | ...` — comment is not a type\n* `classification: str = \"binary\"` on 5+ models\n* `relevance: str = \"direct\"` on 2+ models\n\n### `\"\"` as a `None` sentinel\n\n`model_used: str = \"\"` on multiple contract models. Cannot distinguish \"not set\" from \"set to empty\". Use `str | None = None`.\n\n### Redundant `as_dict()` on frozen dataclasses\n\nManual `as_dict()` methods returning `dict[str, Any]` where `dataclasses.asdict()` works for free.\n\n### Redundant type aliases without `TypeAlias`\n\n`LLMProfileSelection = LLMModelSetSelection` — no `TypeAlias` annotation. Checker cannot distinguish from value assignment.\n\n### Tier enum module-level aliases\n\n`TIER_SMALL = LLMTier.SMALL` etc. — pure noise if callers can import `LLMTier.SMALL`.\n\n### `postprocess(result: Any)` on typed tools\n\nSilent empty-dict fallback if `result` is unexpected type. Should raise; should be typed.\n\n---\n\n## Database / SQLAlchemy / async correctness (deep audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### Pervasive N+1 queries\n\n* `sql_repositories.py:851,856,861,873,887,892,897,912,927,1221,1222` — `_node_row_to_record` / `_edge_row_to_record` loops issue `session.get(...)` per row via `_load_node_type_detail` and `_load_edge_type_detail`. **No** `selectinload`**/**`joinedload` **anywhere in the codebase** (verified via grep).\n* `sql_repositories.py:470-471` — `_graph_row_to_record` runs 2 queries per graph, called from a list comprehension. 1 + 2\\*G pattern.\n* `sql_repositories.py:1236-1240` — `save_graphs` does `session.get(NodeRow, nid)` per node ID per graph.\n\nThis is the single biggest performance liability. At scale it will be catastrophic.\n\n### Whole-table scans to build lookups\n\n`sql_repositories.py:1260,1274` — `session.scalars(select(NodeRow)).all()` loads every row in the table to build a dict.\n\n### Delete-all-then-rewrite as the save model\n\n`save_nodes`, `save_edges`, `save_graph_store_bundle` (`sql_repositories.py:1261-1266, 1273-1278, 1384-1416`) issue `delete(...)` against every row then re-add. Catastrophic transaction sizes; serializes concurrent reads.\n\n### `create_all()` inside repository constructors\n\n`sql_repositories.py:824,1585,1617,1685`, `qc/store.py:69`, `qc/gating_store.py:53`, `qc/research_defensibility_store.py:52`. DDL runs every time a repository is instantiated. Schema bootstrap in read path.\n\n### Schema drift risk: `create_all` + Alembic\n\n`database.py:2435-2440` runs `Base.metadata.create_all(...)` for SQLite, alongside Alembic for Postgres. SQLite schema can drift from Alembic migrations silently. Also `database.py:2469-2471` runs raw `CREATE SEQUENCE IF NOT EXISTS` outside any migration.\n\n### Process-global `_DATABASE_CACHE` is fork-unsafe\n\n`database.py:2489-2502` — engines cached by URL with no eviction, no `dispose()`, no fork awareness. Pre-fork instantiation → child processes share parent's pooled FDs. Classic SQLAlchemy fork-safety bug.\n\n### Connection pool defaults baked in\n\n`database.py:2428-2432` — `pool_size=10`, `max_overflow=20`, `pool_pre_ping=True`, **no** `pool_recycle`. Long-idle connections hit DB-side timeouts, recovered only via pre-ping round-trip.\n\n### SQLite `check_same_thread=False` without serialization discipline\n\n`database.py:2424-2425` plus `ThreadPoolExecutor` (`research_driver_batch_pipeline.py:631`) → concurrent writes against one SQLite connection. No `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`.\n\n### ORM objects leak past `session_scope`\n\n`services/api/routes/graphs.py:181-206` — Pydantic responses built inside `with db.session_scope()` work only because `expire_on_commit=False`. Any access of a non-eagerly-loaded relationship after commit raises `DetachedInstanceError`. Latent bomb.\n\n### Sessions silently committing on reads\n\n`database.py:2477-2483` — `session_scope` *always* commits on clean exit. No read-only counterpart. Every read path produces spurious WAL writes / fsyncs on SQLite.\n\n### `relationship_description` column overloaded as JSON blob\n\n`sql_repositories.py:660-671, 736-751` — `EdgeRow.relationship_description` typed as `Text` but used as JSON serializer. Queries against these fields impossible.\n\n### Sync I/O blocking event loop in async API routes\n\n* `services/api/routes/documents.py:388-465` (`upload_question_documents`) — sync SQLAlchemy + JSON file reads + `write_upload_file` (sync FS) + `sha256_bytes` (CPU), all inside `async def`. Every byte of every upload blocks the event loop.\n* Same pattern at `documents.py:740-807` (`upload_historic_series`).\n* `services/api/app.py:62-71` — `lifespan` calls sync `httpx.Client` request, blocking startup.\n\n**Zero occurrences of** `run_in_executor`**,** `asyncio.to_thread`**, or** `anyio.to_thread` in the codebase. The codebase chose neither async nor sync discipline — it's a hybrid that's wrong both ways.\n\n### `future.cancel()` after timeout doesn't actually cancel\n\n`services/worker/queue_runner.py:190` — Python futures only cancel before they start. The worker leaks until the underlying blocking call completes naturally.\n\n### Resource leaks\n\n* `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/research_agent/http_client.py:36-37` — `requests.Session()` in `__init__` with no `close()`/`__exit__`. Every client instance leaks its connection pool.\n* `pdf_download.py:78-90` — `tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False)` with cleanup in `finally` that can `NameError` if the constructor itself raised; cleanup is then silently swallowed.\n* `tools/cli/commands.py:710,779` — `subprocess.Popen` without `with`.\n\n### Time/datetime correctness\n\n* `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/insight_agent/reduce.py:90` — `datetime.utcnow()` (deprecated in Python 3.12+, returns naive)\n* `packages/domain/qc/gating_store.py:61,341` — `datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat()` snaps to **local** timezone, not UTC\n* `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/research_agent/tiered_retrieval.py:29` — `_CURRENT_YEAR = datetime.now().year` frozen at import; won't roll over on New Year\n* `services/worker/research_driver_question_pipeline.py:1490` — `run_id = f\"{question_id}-{int(time.time())}\"`. Not monotonic; collides within the same second.\n* `services/worker/shared_tools/web_tools.py:574` — `int(time.time())` as filename salt; same problem.\n\nHand-rolled `+00:00 → Z` replace pattern repeated 10+ times across `services/api/`, `packages/storage/sql_repositories.py:196`, `packages/domain/baselines/apply.py:97`. Should be one helper.\n\n### Magic values\n\n* Hardcoded thread-pool sizes: `retrieval_packs.py:2195` (4), `driver_indicator_literature.py:366` (3), `research_driver_question_pipeline.py:1733,2500` (2), `agent.py:850,1111` (4). No central knob.\n* Hardcoded retry/backoff/timeout: `http_client.py:44-46` plus `pdf_download.py:114-145` re-implements with the same constants. No shared retry policy.\n* `_RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES = frozenset({429,500,502,503,504})` defined twice in two different files.\n* `_STATUS_MAP` in `sql_repositories.py:960-968` uses raw strings even though `RunStatus` enum exists in `database.py:169-174`.\n* `raise SystemExit(1)` from `services/api/litellm_check.py:47,58,68` — kills the process from inside an imported function, called from FastAPI lifespan.\n* Hardcoded UA: `pdf_download.py:111` — `\"Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; ElijahBot/1.0)\"`.\n\n---\n\n## Observability, errors, config, tests (deep audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### Logging discipline — the JSON logger is dead in production\n\n`configure_logging` **is only called by** `services/api/app.py:44` **(with** `json_output=False`**). The worker entry path (**`tools/cli/commands.py:489`**) never calls it.** Every `logger.info(...)` from worker code falls back to Python's default WARNING root handler — INFO logs are lost, no JSON ever emitted. The whole structured-log setup is theatre for the worker.\n\n**Three competing logging setups:**\n\n* `core/logging.py:configure_logging` (JSON)\n* `core/utils.py:92 _setup_logging` (basicConfig + file handler)\n* `core/research/tool_cli.py:187 logging.basicConfig`\n\n`core/utils.py:_setup_logging` is called from `main.py:50` and `tools/cli/main.py:50`, stomping any `configure_logging`-installed handlers. Whichever runs last wins.\n\n**Other logging issues:**\n\n* `_CONFIGURED` global makes the logger uninitialisable in tests (`core/logging.py:74`)\n* `set_trace_id`, `set_run_context` only call `.set()` with no `Token`/`reset()` — `run_id` from a prior job leaks into the next one in pooled workers\n* Only 3 files use `extra={...}` for structured logging; \\~95% of call sites use positional `%s`\n* `litellm`/`httpx` muted to WARNING in two different places with no coordination\n* `tools/cli/commands.py:488,493,498,500` and `tools/cli/display.py` use `print()` for operational output that should be logs\n\n### Error message quality\n\n* **207 untyped** `raise RuntimeError`**/**`raise ValueError` in `packages/`, `core/`, `services/`. Storage layer is the worst: `document_store.py`, `fs2_state_store.py:136-444` (\\~25 raises), `prompt_trace_store.py`.\n* `DomainError` **hierarchy** in `core/errors.py:16` (`NotFoundError`, `ConflictError`, `ValidationError`, `PipelineError`) is good — but only used by `services/api/`. Worker and core pipelines raise raw `ValueError`.\n* `packages/inference/llm/runtime.py:614,675,717` — `raise RuntimeError(\"Unreachable: ...\")`. Should be `assert False` or a custom internal-error class.\n* `AttributeError` **raised as contract violation** in `agent.py:212` — can't be distinguished from a real attribute miss.\n* **232 bare** `except Exception` **blocks** in `core/packages/services`. `core/gjopen/scraper.py:372-387` swallows 4 in a row with no logging.\n\n**Good example to emulate:** `packages/storage/database.py:2451-2463` (`StorageSchemaNotReady`) and `core/document_ingest.py:51,61,71,81` (`MarkItDownIngestError`) are exemplary — typed, chained via `from exc`, message includes path + recovery command.\n\n### Configuration management\n\n* **Hand-rolled dotenv** at `core/settings.py:19` parses with `str.partition(\"=\")`. No quoting, no multi-line, no `export`, no `${VAR}` expansion. Breaks on `KEY=value=foo`. **Use** `pydantic-settings`**.**\n* `ENV_VARS` **registry is documentation only** (`core/settings.py:185-212`) — 22 env vars listed with descriptions but no startup validation, no type registry, no parser registry.\n* **Config scattered across 4 surfaces:** `core/settings.py` constants, `config/*.toml` files, `os.environ` reads in 35+ files outside settings, per-module `_FLAG = env_text(...)` reads.\n* **Feature flags as ad-hoc env strings:** `ELIJAH_LLM_PRIORS`, `_FERMI_BASELINE_FLAG`, `_FERMI_EDGE_FLAG`, `_FS11_LLM_FLAG`. Every one is its own `os.environ.get(...).strip().lower() in (\"1\",\"true\",\"yes\",\"on\")` cargo-cult.\n* **No secret discipline:** `ELIJAH_API_KEY`, `LITELLM_API_KEY`, `LITELLM_MASTER_KEY` are in the same dict as `OBJECT_STORE_PREFIX`. No `SecretStr`, no redaction policy. `ApiConfig` is a plain `dataclass(frozen=True)` — anything that logs it dumps the key in plaintext.\n* `_DATABASE_CACHE` is a mutable module-level singleton, leaks across test runs.\n* `_load_dotenv_if_present` **mutates** `os.environ` **at import time.** Test isolation breaks.\n\n### Test quality\n\n* **Zero** `pytest-asyncio` **usage.** API is FastAPI (asyncio-native) but no tests exercise async lifespan, cancellation, or concurrent request paths.\n* **Patching private symbols:** `tests/test_pdf_download.py:21` patches `pdf_download._download_bytes`. `tests/test_analyst_review.py:91-299` uses `MagicMock(spec=...)` 7+ times per file. Tests the implementation, not the contract.\n* **Tests that just verify the mock was called:** `tests/test_litellm_check.py:155` — proves the test setup works, not the system.\n* `SimpleNamespace` **for domain stubs** — `tests/test_iw_trigger.py:34-90`, `tests/test_bn_inference.py:11-79` build graphs out of `SimpleNamespace`, bypassing validation. Tests can construct states production code rejects.\n* `tests/test_indicator_graph_apply.py` **is 1,548 lines** — kitchen-sink integration test.\n* **Only 24** `@pytest.mark.parametrize` **across 148 test files** — similar tests copy-pasted instead.\n* **PG-specific behaviour untested** — `_bootstrap_id_sequences`, ENUMs, FK cascades only run against Postgres, never tested.\n\n### Pydantic / dataclass consistency\n\n* **119 BaseModel, 133 @dataclass, 2 TypedDict** — and no `docs/CONVENTIONS.md` documenting which to use when.\n* `model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\"ignore\")` **everywhere** on LLM output models (`research/question_domains.py:105,114`, `iw_verdict_tool.py:32,99`, `indicator_extraction.py:123,134`). **Silently drops unknown fields from LLM output.** When the LLM adds a field, you discover it via a missing-data bug, not a validation error. Should be `extra=\"forbid\"` for LLM outputs.\n* `frozen=True` **inconsistent:** 113 of 133 dataclasses are frozen. The non-frozen ones include domain types in `core/pipelines/graph_persistence.py:41`, `core/research_driver/data_model.py:80,93`.\n* **TypedDict used exactly twice** — should either be `BaseModel` (validated) or `dict[str, Any]` (honest).\n\n### Dependency injection vs globals\n\n* `default_prompt_store()` **is** `@cache`**-decorated** at `packages/inference/prompts/store.py:37`. 12+ call sites do `default_prompt_store().load(\"...\")` inline. Hidden global state — tests must monkeypatch the module attribute.\n* **Two** `default_prompt_store` **functions:** the canonical one and a shadow at `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/prompt_store.py:30` with different render mode and env var. Greppers find either.\n* `_ACTIVE_MODEL_SET: ContextVar` + env var fallback in `tiers.py:42,86,121-146` — two parallel injection channels with buried precedence rules.\n\n**Good pattern that should be applied everywhere:** `WorkerStorageAdapters` (`packages/storage/worker.py:42`) is a `@dataclass(frozen=True)` of repositories passed through `create_app(storage_adapters=...)`. Proper DI. The contrast with `default_prompt_store()` shows the team knows the right pattern.\n\n---\n\n## Security surface (deep audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### SSRF via user-controlled URLs\n\n`services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/research_agent/retrieval_packs.py` and `http_client.py` fetch URLs derived from search results and user-supplied input. No allowlist, no blocked-IP-range check (RFC 1918, loopback, link-local), no scheme validation. An adversary who controls a search result or question URL can direct the worker to internal services (`http://169.254.169.254/`, `http://localhost:5432/`, etc.).\n\n### Secrets in logs / plaintext config\n\n`ApiConfig` is a plain frozen dataclass. Any `logger.info(f\"Starting with config {config}\")` dumps `ELIJAH_API_KEY`, `LITELLM_MASTER_KEY` in plaintext. No `SecretStr`, no `__repr__` override, no redaction filter on handlers. The structured log setup makes this worse — when JSON logging is eventually wired into the worker, every config-log line will be machine-readable credential exfiltration.\n\n### `extra=\"ignore\"` on LLM response models as a prompt-injection vector\n\nLLM output models silently drop unknown fields (`extra=\"ignore\"`). A jailbroken or compromised LLM returning `{\"action\": \"delete_all\", ...}` passes validation — the extra field is silently discarded. Switching to `extra=\"forbid\"` makes unexpected fields a hard error, surfacing prompt injection attempts rather than silently laundering them.\n\n### No authentication or authorisation model documented\n\nThe FastAPI app has no visible middleware for auth (`services/api/app.py`). No JWT validation, no API key check, no rate limiting. If this is intentional (internal-only service), it is not documented. An investor or auditor will ask; the answer needs to be explicit.\n\n### `subprocess.Popen` without shell=False enforcement\n\n`tools/cli/commands.py:710,779` — `Popen` without `with` and without verifying `shell=False`. If any argument is ever constructed from user input, this becomes command injection. Currently appears safe, but the pattern is fragile.\n\n### Hardcoded user-agent string\n\n`pdf_download.py:111` — `\"Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; ElijahBot/1.0)\"`. Not a security vulnerability per se, but discloses the bot identity and product name to every third-party server the worker touches.\n\n---\n\n## Dependency hygiene (audit checklist, 2026-05-12)\n\n### CVE scan not in CI\n\n`pip-audit` / `uv run pip-audit` is not in the CI pipeline. Known CVEs in dependencies go undetected until manually checked. Run: `uv run pip-audit` and add it to the pre-push checklist.\n\n### Floating version pins\n\n`pyproject.toml` uses `>=` lower bounds for most dependencies. Any `uv lock --upgrade` can silently pull in a breaking minor release or a CVE-carrying patch. Pin transitive dependencies via the lockfile and verify upgrades explicitly. At minimum, review the lockfile diff on every dependency bump PR.\n\n### LiteLLM pinning\n\nLiteLLM releases multiple times per week and has had breaking changes in minor versions. The version constraint should be pinned to a specific minor (e.g., `==1.x.y`) and upgraded deliberately, not opportunistically.\n\n---\n\n## Operational readiness (audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### No metrics endpoint\n\nThe API has no `/metrics` (Prometheus) or equivalent. There is no way to scrape request latency, queue depth, LLM call counts, or error rates. Observability is currently log-only, and — as noted above — worker logs are largely invisible. An investor asking \"how do you monitor production?\" has no answer from the code.\n\n### Single health check — litellm only\n\n`services/api/litellm_check.py` checks LiteLLM connectivity at startup. There is no ongoing `/health` or `/readiness` endpoint that checks DB connectivity, queue connectivity, or object-store reachability. Container orchestrators (k8s, ECS) cannot distinguish \"process alive\" from \"process serving traffic correctly\".\n\n### No alerting story\n\nNo integration with PagerDuty, Opsgenie, or equivalent. No `Sentry` / `Honeybadger` / `Rollbar` error tracking. Errors surface only if someone reads the logs. For a product claiming prediction accuracy, silent failures in the research pipeline are a business risk.\n\n### Worker has no graceful shutdown\n\n`services/worker/queue_runner.py` has a `future.cancel()` that does not actually cancel in-flight work (noted above). There is no SIGTERM handler that drains the current job before exit. Kubernetes pod eviction mid-research-run produces a partially-written graph with no recovery path.\n\n### No structured runbook\n\nNo `docs/runbook.md` or equivalent. An on-call engineer hitting an incident has no documented procedure. For investor due diligence, the absence of operational documentation is a flag.\n\n---\n\n## API contract stability (audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### No versioned endpoints\n\nAll routes are at `/` or `/api/...` with no version prefix (`/v1/`). Any breaking change to a response schema silently breaks callers. There is no deprecation story. If a third party (demo partner, investor sandbox) integrates against the API, a schema change has no migration path.\n\n### No schema registry\n\nThe Pydantic DTOs in `packages/contracts/` serve as the implicit API schema, but they are not published or versioned. There is no OpenAPI schema pinned in CI (the auto-generated `/openapi.json` changes with every model change). Breaking changes are invisible until a client fails at runtime.\n\n### `data: dict[str, Any]` on `QuestionRead`\n\nThe primary question read DTO carries a freeform `dict[str, Any]` field. This makes the API contract impossible to version or document precisely. Any typed field added inside `data` is invisible to OpenAPI and undetectable by clients without runtime inspection.\n\n### No changelog or deprecation policy\n\nNo `CHANGELOG.md`, no `DEPRECATED` markers on removed fields, no migration guide. This is acceptable for a pre-launch internal API but must be in place before any external integration.\n\n---\n\n## Production runtime telemetry (queried 2026-05-12, Betty / Postgres)\n\nQueried directly from the production Postgres database on Betty (`elijah` DB). All data is from the system's entire operational lifetime.\n\n### Lifetime job volume\n\n| Metric | Value |\n| -- | -- |\n| Total jobs ever run | 6 |\n| Date range | 2026-05-07 only |\n| Completed jobs | 0 |\n| Partial jobs | 1 |\n| Failed jobs | 5 (83%) |\n\nThe system has never produced a completed job. The single successful `run` record was produced by the `partial` job.\n\n### Failure breakdown (all 5 failed jobs)\n\n| Root cause | Count | Notes |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| Missing `question.data.domain` field | 2 | Config/data error; not a code bug |\n| Silent failure — no error recorded | 1 | **Confirms bare** `except` **swallowing in production** |\n| Worker restart orphaned job | 1 | **Confirms graceful-shutdown gap in production** |\n| Worker stuck — no LLM calls reaching LiteLLM proxy | 1 | Connectivity or config issue |\n\n### The one successful run — step timing\n\n| Step | Duration |\n| -- | -- |\n| `indicator_extraction` | 233.7s |\n| `extraction` | 172.7s |\n| `research` | 142.1s |\n| `driver_indicator_literature` | 123.1s |\n| `inference` | 0.5s |\n| `setup` / `priors` / `report` | \\~0s each |\n| **Total wall time** | **\\~11.25 minutes** |\n\n### LLM cost tracking is completely broken\n\n`runs.total_llm_calls`, `runs.total_llm_tokens`, `runs.total_llm_usd` are all NULL on the completed run. `run_steps.llm_calls`, `llm_input_tokens`, `llm_output_tokens`, `llm_usd`, `llm_by_model` are all NULL for every step. The schema exists to capture this; the code never writes to it.\n\nLiteLLM's own spend log (`LiteLLM_SpendLogs`) shows only 1 call for $0.0001 from 2026-04-23 — the research pipeline's LLM calls are entirely absent. **There is currently no way to know what a research run costs.**\n\n### Other instrumentation gaps confirmed\n\n* `runs.git_branch` and `runs.app_version` are NULL — no build provenance on runs\n* `runs.total_seconds` is NULL on the completed run despite `run_steps` having individual durations — the rollup is never computed\n* `job_events` has 41 `progress` events, 6 `claimed`, 3 `failed`, 1 `completed` — event spine exists but is not surfaced anywhere\n\n### Artifact inventory (from the one successful run)\n\n| Kind | Count | Size |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| `graph_objects` | 1 | 332 KB |\n| `audit_summary` | 2 | 71 KB |\n| `drivers` | 2 | 101 KB |\n| `sources` | 2 | 59 KB |\n| `run_summary_json` | 2 | 29 KB |\n| `indicators_graph` | 1 | 29 KB |\n| `qc_index` | 4 | 22 KB |\n| `driver_indicators` | 1 | 23 KB |\n| `report` | 2 | 12 KB |\n| `question_summary` | 1 | 5 KB |\n| `run_summary_text` | 2 | 4 KB |\n| `artifacts_index` | 2 | 7 KB |\n| `other` | 2 | 3 KB |\n\n### Summary assessment\n\nThe production database confirms the three most urgent audit findings are not theoretical — silent exception swallowing and missing graceful shutdown have already caused job failures. LLM cost tracking being entirely unwritten means the team has no data on what the system costs to operate.\n\n---\n\n## Approach\n\nReference `lookout.md` as the authoritative list. Highest-impact items before any investor technical review:\n\n 1. Wire `configure_logging` into the worker entry (`tools/cli/commands.py:489`) — otherwise the structured-log infrastructure is theatre\n 2. **Fix LLM cost tracking** — write to `runs.total_llm_calls/usd` and `run_steps.llm_*` fields; currently all NULL and the system has no cost visibility\n 3. Strip the `Role: / Where It Is Used:` boilerplate from module docstrings\n 4. Define a `GraphLike` Protocol — eliminates `graph: Any` cascade\n 5. Fix sync I/O inside async API route handlers (`documents.py:388-465, 740-807`) — event-loop blocker\n 6. Add `selectinload`/`joinedload` discipline in `sql_repositories.py` — kill the pervasive N+1\n 7. Move `create_all()` out of repository constructors to application startup\n 8. Replace `extra=\"ignore\"` with `extra=\"forbid\"` on LLM output models — silent field drop is a debugging nightmare and a prompt-injection blind spot\n 9. Replace `datetime.utcnow()` with `datetime.now(UTC)`; replace `datetime.now().astimezone()` in `gating_store.py` with `datetime.now(UTC)`\n10. Replace `requests.Session()` instances with context-managed clients\n11. Adopt `pydantic-settings` for `core/settings.py`; consolidate the 35+ scattered `os.environ` reads\n12. Apply `SecretStr` to API keys in `ApiConfig` and related — prevent plaintext key logging\n13. Add SSRF protection to the URL-fetching paths in `retrieval_packs.py` / `http_client.py`\n14. Add SIGTERM graceful-shutdown handler to the worker — already caused a job failure in production\n15. Use the `DomainError` hierarchy in workers and core pipelines, not just the API\n16. Replace `RuntimeError(\"Unreachable: ...\")` with `assert False` or typed internal-error\n17. Replace `str` with `StrEnum`/`Literal` on contract fields (`classification`, `relevance`, `target_type`, etc.)\n18. Replace `\"\"` as `None` sentinel with `str | None = None`\n19. Clean storage mapper defensive wrapping (most visible per-file smell)\n20. Break up `_run_single_step_impl()` and `run_research_driver_for_question()`\n21. Extract config dataclass from 45-parameter `run_research_driver_over_questions()`\n22. Refactor `retrieval_packs.py` copy-pasted classes\n23. Extract base class for the three `fermi_*.py` files\n24. Split `packages/storage/__init__.py` barrel alongside `sql_repositories.py`\n25. Add a centralised retry policy / thread pool configuration; remove magic constants\n26. Document `Pydantic` vs `dataclass` vs `TypedDict` convention in `docs/CONVENTIONS.md`\n27. Add `pip-audit` to CI and pin LiteLLM to a specific minor version\n28. Add a `/health` readiness endpoint that checks DB + queue reachability\n29. Add `/v1/` prefix to all API routes and pin the OpenAPI schema in CI\n\nSee also: SPI-254 for the documentation gaps follow-up.\n\n## Tooling\n\n* `uvx vulture packages/ core/ services/ --min-confidence 80` — dead code\n* `uv run ruff check . --select F401` — unused imports\n* `uv run mypy --strict` — would surface most `Any` / `Optional` / missing return type issues (not in CI)\n* `uv run pip-audit` — CVE scan against locked dependencies\n* Add `pytest-asyncio` and tests exercising async lifecycle / cancellation\n* Add `pytest-httpserver` to replace mock-based HTTP tests\n* Add a semgrep rule for `datetime.utcnow()` and `datetime.now()` without timezone\n* Add a semgrep rule for `os.environ` outside `core/settings.py`\n* Add a semgrep rule for `requests.Session()` without context manager","priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/spire-elijah/issue/SPI-252/code-quality-sweep-remove-dead-code-defensive-wrapping-and-env-var","gitBranchName":"sumeet/spi-252-code-quality-sweep-remove-dead-code-defensive-wrapping-and","createdAt":"2026-05-12T14:55:23.345Z","updatedAt":"2026-05-12T19:36:08.719Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":[],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"sumeet@sumeetsaini.com","createdById":"c7a8f74f-a7ba-41e8-b771-85fc97b42216","assignee":"sumeet@sumeetsaini.com","assigneeId":"c7a8f74f-a7ba-41e8-b771-85fc97b42216","project":"Elijah Pipeline Development","projectId":"cf9fb1a1-0344-45e5-933a-a689a00d3858","team":"Spire_Elijah","teamId":"6fc98d18-8a80-4c49-8c42-a6e2a4d3d722"}

{
  "id": "SPI-252",
  "description": "## What this is\n\nSystematic sweep of the codebase for anti-patterns documented in `lookout.md`. The <issue id=\"15c6f655-f34e-4a27-8fff-d068f32f5913\">SPI-228</issue> port review surfaced several instances, but these patterns likely exist elsewhere too.\n\n## Anti-patterns to scan for\n\n1. **Dead code** — functions defined but never called, left behind with \"compatibility\" or \"legacy\" comments. Grep for callers; if none, delete.\n2. **Defensive** `str(x or \"\").strip()` **wrapping** — on values that are already typed strings. Adds noise, no safety benefit.\n3. `os.environ` **deep in domain code** — env vars read inside domain functions instead of at config/CLI edges.\n4. **Module-level model resolution constants** — `MY_MODEL = resolve_task_model(...)` at import time.\n5. **Inline system prompts** — hardcoded prompt strings instead of loading from `prompts/` via the prompt store.\n6. **Global mutation lazy imports** — `global _x` with `Any | None` sentinels instead of function-scoped imports.\n\n## Known instances (from <issue id=\"15c6f655-f34e-4a27-8fff-d068f32f5913\">SPI-228</issue> review)\n\n* `extract_causal_relationships()` in `indicator_extraction.py` — dead, never called (<issue id=\"f7cb8aa6-b26a-44c0-857c-aa627b2d4c73\">SPI-250</issue>)\n* `os.environ` in `_build_full_message_list()` in `indicator_extraction.py` (<issue id=\"f7cb8aa6-b26a-44c0-857c-aa627b2d4c73\">SPI-250</issue>)\n* Circular import lazy globals in `driver_indicator_literature.py` (<issue id=\"7ef805df-0d80-43d1-9edf-68379eddcfcc\">SPI-249</issue>)\n\n## Codebase scan findings (2026-05-12)\n\n### Defensive wrapping — 286 hits across non-test code\n\n`str(x or \"\")` pattern concentrated in storage mappers:\n\n* `packages/domain/qc/gating_store.py` — 14 instances\n* `packages/storage/sql_repositories.py` — 12 instances\n* `core/research_driver/data_model.py` — 12 instances\n* `packages/domain/qc/store.py` — 11 instances\n\nConfirmed at line level: function signatures declare `run_id: str` and the body does `str(run_id or \"\").strip()`. Type says `str`, code treats as `str | None | Any`. Fix the boundary, not the interior.\n\n`dict(x or {})` wrapping: ~45 hits across 20 files. Identity list comprehensions: 4 files.\n\n### `os.environ` in domain code — 16 files\n\nOutside config/settings/cli: `llm_scorer.py`, `relationship_judgment.py`, `fermi_llm.py`, `conditional_edge_quality.py`, `question_summary_pipeline.py`, etc.\n\n### Global mutation sentinels — 18 instances\n\nMostly `packages/inference/llm/runtime.py` (likely intentional lazy loading) but also `llm_scorer.py`, `fermi_llm.py`, `llm_prior.py`.\n\n### Module-level `resolve_task_model` — 2 remaining\n\n* `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/research_agent/web_search_client.py:37`\n* `core/research_driver/indicator_extraction.py:44`\n\n### Inline system prompts — 2 instances\n\n* `research/question_domains.py:132`\n* `core/pipelines/question_set_evaluation.py:257`\n\nNote: `core/research_driver/conditional_edge_quality.py` hardcodes its prompt while the sibling `FermiConditionalEdgeQualityTool` in the same file correctly loads from the prompt store.\n\n### Zero-coverage files with real logic (from pytest --cov)\n\nOverall coverage: 78%. Files at 0% that are not CLI/HTTP-client:\n\n| File | Stmts | Notes |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| `core/pipelines/question_set_evaluation.py` | 497 | May be superseded |\n| `packages/domain/qc/research_defensibility_store.py` | 117 | Domain storage |\n| `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/research_agent/web_search_client.py` | 138 | May be superseded |\n| `packages/domain/qc/track_record_loader.py` | 73 | Track record feature |\n| `packages/domain/qc/track_record_aware_judge.py` | 47 | Track record feature |\n| `fs10_validation/components/fs3_extraction.py` | 97 | Validation component |\n| `services/worker/run_config_context.py` | 17 | Nothing imports this |\n\n`track_record_loader` / `track_record_aware_judge` at 0% coverage with no callers — likely abandoned.\n\n### Backward-compat shims — 17 instances\n\nExplicit \"backward compat\" / \"will be removed\" comments in:\n\n* `packages/storage/database.py` lines 670, 2530, 2640\n* `packages/storage/graph_store.py` lines 334, 380\n* `core/pipelines/graph_persistence.py:602`\n* `core/research_driver/indicator_extraction.py:587`\n* `core/research_driver/question_workflow.py:23`\n* `core/research_driver/driver_ontology.py:316`\n* `services/worker/research_driver_question_pipeline.py:641`\n* `packages/domain/baselines/apply.py:268`\n\n### AI-boilerplate module docstrings — 103 files\n\n`Role: / Where It Is Used: / Relies On: / Key Symbols:` docstring format. Single most visible AI-generation tell. Replace with one concise sentence per module.\n\n### Monster functions\n\n| Function | File | Size |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| `_run_single_step_impl()` | `services/worker/research_driver_step_pipeline.py:404` | 1,416 lines |\n| `run_research_driver_for_question()` | `services/worker/research_driver_question_pipeline.py:1861` | 1,205 lines |\n| `factor_extraction.run()` | `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/insight_agent/factor_extraction.py:731` | 533 lines |\n\n`run_research_driver_over_questions()` in `research_driver_batch_pipeline.py:396` takes **45 parameters**.\n\n### God files\n\n| File | Lines |\n| -- | -- |\n| `retrieval_packs.py` | 3,380 |\n| `research_driver_question_pipeline.py` | 3,066 |\n| `database.py` | 2,643 |\n| `sql_repositories.py` | 2,015 |\n| `research_driver_step_pipeline.py` | 1,957 |\n\n`retrieval_packs.py` also has 33 local imports.\n\n### Duplication\n\n* `retrieval_packs.py` internal copy-pasted classes (`assess_candidate()`, `build_query()`, `search_candidates()`, `validate_scope_details()`, `iter_sources()` each 4–7 times in the same file)\n* `fermi_target.py` / `fermi_driver.py` / `fermi_indicator.py` structurally identical\n\nNote: `build_messages()` / `postprocess()` across tool files are `BaseLLMTool` protocol implementations — correct polymorphism, not duplication.\n\n### Files that should be split\n\n* `core/research/historical_data.py` (834 lines) → split by fetcher type\n* `services/api/mappers.py` (755 lines) → split by domain (question / graph / job)\n* `core/run_config.py` (683 lines) → split by config domain\n* `packages/inference/llm/tiers.py` (610 lines) → types / loading / resolution\n* `packages/storage/sql_repositories.py` (2,015 lines) → question_repository / graph_repository\n\n### `__init__.py` barrel files\n\n`packages/storage/__init__.py` is 455 lines, re-exporting everything. Increases circular import risk and inflates the import graph. Fix alongside the `sql_repositories.py` split. Others are fine.\n\n### `__all__` in non-`__init__` files\n\n`calibration_sweep.py` (~41 exports), `retrieval_packs.py` (~24) — downstream of the god-file problem.\n\n### Enums\n\nClean. Only `ArtifactKind` (13 values) has 10+ values.\n\n---\n\n## Type system issues (deep review findings, 2026-05-12)\n\n### The `graph: Any` gap — single most consequential issue\n\nCentral graph domain object passed as `Any` everywhere (`qc/store.py:215`, `conditional_edge_quality.py:240`, dozens more). Every `getattr(graph, \"run_id\", \"\")` is untyped dispatch. A `GraphLike(Protocol)` eliminates dozens of `getattr` calls and enables checker enforcement.\n\n### `dict[str, Any]` on domain dataclass fields\n\nFields with known shapes typed as `Any` (`qc/store.py`: `input_json`, `axes`, `index_json`; `contracts/questions.py`: `data` on `QuestionRead`; `tiers.py` cache returns). Use `TypedDict` or Pydantic models.\n\n### Import-time filesystem side effects\n\n`SYSTEM_PROMPT = _load_system_prompt()` at module level in all three Fermi files. Importing the module unconditionally hits the filesystem. Lazy-load with `@lru_cache` instead.\n\n### `_ensure_database()` called inside read functions\n\n`qc/store.py` calls `_ensure_database()` (which runs `create_all()`) inside every read. DDL on read paths is wrong. Schema init belongs at startup.\n\n### `assert` as a runtime guard\n\n`assert stored is not None` in `qc/store.py:152`. Disabled with `-O`. Use `raise RuntimeError(...)` or a typed result.\n\n### `except Exception` with swallowed errors\n\n`conditional_edge_quality.py`: bare `except Exception` converting real errors to dict entries, marked `# pragma: no cover`. Silent failure path.\n\n### Private symbol imports across modules\n\n`from packages.storage.graph_store import _edges_payload, _write_json` — couples to internal implementation.\n\n### Truthiness bug in Fermi files\n\n`f\"{horizon_days} days\" if horizon_days else (horizon_bucket or \"unknown\")` — if `horizon_days = 0`, falsy branch wrongly fires. Use `is not None`.\n\n### `str` where `Literal`/`StrEnum` belongs — contracts\n\n* `target_type: str  # \"binary\" | \"categorical\" | ...` — comment is not a type\n* `classification: str = \"binary\"` on 5+ models\n* `relevance: str = \"direct\"` on 2+ models\n\n### `\"\"` as a `None` sentinel\n\n`model_used: str = \"\"` on multiple contract models. Cannot distinguish \"not set\" from \"set to empty\". Use `str | None = None`.\n\n### Redundant `as_dict()` on frozen dataclasses\n\nManual `as_dict()` methods returning `dict[str, Any]` where `dataclasses.asdict()` works for free.\n\n### Redundant type aliases without `TypeAlias`\n\n`LLMProfileSelection = LLMModelSetSelection` — no `TypeAlias` annotation. Checker cannot distinguish from value assignment.\n\n### Tier enum module-level aliases\n\n`TIER_SMALL = LLMTier.SMALL` etc. — pure noise if callers can import `LLMTier.SMALL`.\n\n### `postprocess(result: Any)` on typed tools\n\nSilent empty-dict fallback if `result` is unexpected type. Should raise; should be typed.\n\n---\n\n## Database / SQLAlchemy / async correctness (deep audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### Pervasive N+1 queries\n\n* `sql_repositories.py:851,856,861,873,887,892,897,912,927,1221,1222` — `_node_row_to_record` / `_edge_row_to_record` loops issue `session.get(...)` per row via `_load_node_type_detail` and `_load_edge_type_detail`. **No** `selectinload`**/**`joinedload` **anywhere in the codebase** (verified via grep).\n* `sql_repositories.py:470-471` — `_graph_row_to_record` runs 2 queries per graph, called from a list comprehension. 1 + 2\\*G pattern.\n* `sql_repositories.py:1236-1240` — `save_graphs` does `session.get(NodeRow, nid)` per node ID per graph.\n\nThis is the single biggest performance liability. At scale it will be catastrophic.\n\n### Whole-table scans to build lookups\n\n`sql_repositories.py:1260,1274` — `session.scalars(select(NodeRow)).all()` loads every row in the table to build a dict.\n\n### Delete-all-then-rewrite as the save model\n\n`save_nodes`, `save_edges`, `save_graph_store_bundle` (`sql_repositories.py:1261-1266, 1273-1278, 1384-1416`) issue `delete(...)` against every row then re-add. Catastrophic transaction sizes; serializes concurrent reads.\n\n### `create_all()` inside repository constructors\n\n`sql_repositories.py:824,1585,1617,1685`, `qc/store.py:69`, `qc/gating_store.py:53`, `qc/research_defensibility_store.py:52`. DDL runs every time a repository is instantiated. Schema bootstrap in read path.\n\n### Schema drift risk: `create_all` + Alembic\n\n`database.py:2435-2440` runs `Base.metadata.create_all(...)` for SQLite, alongside Alembic for Postgres. SQLite schema can drift from Alembic migrations silently. Also `database.py:2469-2471` runs raw `CREATE SEQUENCE IF NOT EXISTS` outside any migration.\n\n### Process-global `_DATABASE_CACHE` is fork-unsafe\n\n`database.py:2489-2502` — engines cached by URL with no eviction, no `dispose()`, no fork awareness. Pre-fork instantiation → child processes share parent's pooled FDs. Classic SQLAlchemy fork-safety bug.\n\n### Connection pool defaults baked in\n\n`database.py:2428-2432` — `pool_size=10`, `max_overflow=20`, `pool_pre_ping=True`, **no** `pool_recycle`. Long-idle connections hit DB-side timeouts, recovered only via pre-ping round-trip.\n\n### SQLite `check_same_thread=False` without serialization discipline\n\n`database.py:2424-2425` plus `ThreadPoolExecutor` (`research_driver_batch_pipeline.py:631`) → concurrent writes against one SQLite connection. No `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`.\n\n### ORM objects leak past `session_scope`\n\n`services/api/routes/graphs.py:181-206` — Pydantic responses built inside `with db.session_scope()` work only because `expire_on_commit=False`. Any access of a non-eagerly-loaded relationship after commit raises `DetachedInstanceError`. Latent bomb.\n\n### Sessions silently committing on reads\n\n`database.py:2477-2483` — `session_scope` *always* commits on clean exit. No read-only counterpart. Every read path produces spurious WAL writes / fsyncs on SQLite.\n\n### `relationship_description` column overloaded as JSON blob\n\n`sql_repositories.py:660-671, 736-751` — `EdgeRow.relationship_description` typed as `Text` but used as JSON serializer. Queries against these fields impossible.\n\n### Sync I/O blocking event loop in async API routes\n\n* `services/api/routes/documents.py:388-465` (`upload_question_documents`) — sync SQLAlchemy + JSON file reads + `write_upload_file` (sync FS) + `sha256_bytes` (CPU), all inside `async def`. Every byte of every upload blocks the event loop.\n* Same pattern at `documents.py:740-807` (`upload_historic_series`).\n* `services/api/app.py:62-71` — `lifespan` calls sync `httpx.Client` request, blocking startup.\n\n**Zero occurrences of** `run_in_executor`**,** `asyncio.to_thread`**, or** `anyio.to_thread` in the codebase. The codebase chose neither async nor sync discipline — it's a hybrid that's wrong both ways.\n\n### `future.cancel()` after timeout doesn't actually cancel\n\n`services/worker/queue_runner.py:190` — Python futures only cancel before they start. The worker leaks until the underlying blocking call completes naturally.\n\n### Resource leaks\n\n* `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/research_agent/http_client.py:36-37` — `requests.Session()` in `__init__` with no `close()`/`__exit__`. Every client instance leaks its connection pool.\n* `pdf_download.py:78-90` — `tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False)` with cleanup in `finally` that can `NameError` if the constructor itself raised; cleanup is then silently swallowed.\n* `tools/cli/commands.py:710,779` — `subprocess.Popen` without `with`.\n\n### Time/datetime correctness\n\n* `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/insight_agent/reduce.py:90` — `datetime.utcnow()` (deprecated in Python 3.12+, returns naive)\n* `packages/domain/qc/gating_store.py:61,341` — `datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat()` snaps to **local** timezone, not UTC\n* `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/research_agent/tiered_retrieval.py:29` — `_CURRENT_YEAR = datetime.now().year` frozen at import; won't roll over on New Year\n* `services/worker/research_driver_question_pipeline.py:1490` — `run_id = f\"{question_id}-{int(time.time())}\"`. Not monotonic; collides within the same second.\n* `services/worker/shared_tools/web_tools.py:574` — `int(time.time())` as filename salt; same problem.\n\nHand-rolled `+00:00 → Z` replace pattern repeated 10+ times across `services/api/`, `packages/storage/sql_repositories.py:196`, `packages/domain/baselines/apply.py:97`. Should be one helper.\n\n### Magic values\n\n* Hardcoded thread-pool sizes: `retrieval_packs.py:2195` (4), `driver_indicator_literature.py:366` (3), `research_driver_question_pipeline.py:1733,2500` (2), `agent.py:850,1111` (4). No central knob.\n* Hardcoded retry/backoff/timeout: `http_client.py:44-46` plus `pdf_download.py:114-145` re-implements with the same constants. No shared retry policy.\n* `_RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES = frozenset({429,500,502,503,504})` defined twice in two different files.\n* `_STATUS_MAP` in `sql_repositories.py:960-968` uses raw strings even though `RunStatus` enum exists in `database.py:169-174`.\n* `raise SystemExit(1)` from `services/api/litellm_check.py:47,58,68` — kills the process from inside an imported function, called from FastAPI lifespan.\n* Hardcoded UA: `pdf_download.py:111` — `\"Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; ElijahBot/1.0)\"`.\n\n---\n\n## Observability, errors, config, tests (deep audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### Logging discipline — the JSON logger is dead in production\n\n`configure_logging` **is only called by** `services/api/app.py:44` **(with** `json_output=False`**). The worker entry path (**`tools/cli/commands.py:489`**) never calls it.** Every `logger.info(...)` from worker code falls back to Python's default WARNING root handler — INFO logs are lost, no JSON ever emitted. The whole structured-log setup is theatre for the worker.\n\n**Three competing logging setups:**\n\n* `core/logging.py:configure_logging` (JSON)\n* `core/utils.py:92 _setup_logging` (basicConfig + file handler)\n* `core/research/tool_cli.py:187 logging.basicConfig`\n\n`core/utils.py:_setup_logging` is called from `main.py:50` and `tools/cli/main.py:50`, stomping any `configure_logging`-installed handlers. Whichever runs last wins.\n\n**Other logging issues:**\n\n* `_CONFIGURED` global makes the logger uninitialisable in tests (`core/logging.py:74`)\n* `set_trace_id`, `set_run_context` only call `.set()` with no `Token`/`reset()` — `run_id` from a prior job leaks into the next one in pooled workers\n* Only 3 files use `extra={...}` for structured logging; ~95% of call sites use positional `%s`\n* `litellm`/`httpx` muted to WARNING in two different places with no coordination\n* `tools/cli/commands.py:488,493,498,500` and `tools/cli/display.py` use `print()` for operational output that should be logs\n\n### Error message quality\n\n* **207 untyped** `raise RuntimeError`**/**`raise ValueError` in `packages/`, `core/`, `services/`. Storage layer is the worst: `document_store.py`, `fs2_state_store.py:136-444` (~25 raises), `prompt_trace_store.py`.\n* `DomainError` **hierarchy** in `core/errors.py:16` (`NotFoundError`, `ConflictError`, `ValidationError`, `PipelineError`) is good — but only used by `services/api/`. Worker and core pipelines raise raw `ValueError`.\n* `packages/inference/llm/runtime.py:614,675,717` — `raise RuntimeError(\"Unreachable: ...\")`. Should be `assert False` or a custom internal-error class.\n* `AttributeError` **raised as contract violation** in `agent.py:212` — can't be distinguished from a real attribute miss.\n* **232 bare** `except Exception` **blocks** in `core/packages/services`. `core/gjopen/scraper.py:372-387` swallows 4 in a row with no logging.\n\n**Good example to emulate:** `packages/storage/database.py:2451-2463` (`StorageSchemaNotReady`) and `core/document_ingest.py:51,61,71,81` (`MarkItDownIngestError`) are exemplary — typed, chained via `from exc`, message includes path + recovery command.\n\n### Configuration management\n\n* **Hand-rolled dotenv** at `core/settings.py:19` parses with `str.partition(\"=\")`. No quoting, no multi-line, no `export`, no `${VAR}` expansion. Breaks on `KEY=value=foo`. **Use** `pydantic-settings`**.**\n* `ENV_VARS` **registry is documentation only** (`core/settings.py:185-212`) — 22 env vars listed with descriptions but no startup validation, no type registry, no parser registry.\n* **Config scattered across 4 surfaces:** `core/settings.py` constants, `config/*.toml` files, `os.environ` reads in 35+ files outside settings, per-module `_FLAG = env_text(...)` reads.\n* **Feature flags as ad-hoc env strings:** `ELIJAH_LLM_PRIORS`, `_FERMI_BASELINE_FLAG`, `_FERMI_EDGE_FLAG`, `_FS11_LLM_FLAG`. Every one is its own `os.environ.get(...).strip().lower() in (\"1\",\"true\",\"yes\",\"on\")` cargo-cult.\n* **No secret discipline:** `ELIJAH_API_KEY`, `LITELLM_API_KEY`, `LITELLM_MASTER_KEY` are in the same dict as `OBJECT_STORE_PREFIX`. No `SecretStr`, no redaction policy. `ApiConfig` is a plain `dataclass(frozen=True)` — anything that logs it dumps the key in plaintext.\n* `_DATABASE_CACHE` is a mutable module-level singleton, leaks across test runs.\n* `_load_dotenv_if_present` **mutates** `os.environ` **at import time.** Test isolation breaks.\n\n### Test quality\n\n* **Zero** `pytest-asyncio` **usage.** API is FastAPI (asyncio-native) but no tests exercise async lifespan, cancellation, or concurrent request paths.\n* **Patching private symbols:** `tests/test_pdf_download.py:21` patches `pdf_download._download_bytes`. `tests/test_analyst_review.py:91-299` uses `MagicMock(spec=...)` 7+ times per file. Tests the implementation, not the contract.\n* **Tests that just verify the mock was called:** `tests/test_litellm_check.py:155` — proves the test setup works, not the system.\n* `SimpleNamespace` **for domain stubs** — `tests/test_iw_trigger.py:34-90`, `tests/test_bn_inference.py:11-79` build graphs out of `SimpleNamespace`, bypassing validation. Tests can construct states production code rejects.\n* `tests/test_indicator_graph_apply.py` **is 1,548 lines** — kitchen-sink integration test.\n* **Only 24** `@pytest.mark.parametrize` **across 148 test files** — similar tests copy-pasted instead.\n* **PG-specific behaviour untested** — `_bootstrap_id_sequences`, ENUMs, FK cascades only run against Postgres, never tested.\n\n### Pydantic / dataclass consistency\n\n* **119 BaseModel, 133 @dataclass, 2 TypedDict** — and no `docs/CONVENTIONS.md` documenting which to use when.\n* `model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\"ignore\")` **everywhere** on LLM output models (`research/question_domains.py:105,114`, `iw_verdict_tool.py:32,99`, `indicator_extraction.py:123,134`). **Silently drops unknown fields from LLM output.** When the LLM adds a field, you discover it via a missing-data bug, not a validation error. Should be `extra=\"forbid\"` for LLM outputs.\n* `frozen=True` **inconsistent:** 113 of 133 dataclasses are frozen. The non-frozen ones include domain types in `core/pipelines/graph_persistence.py:41`, `core/research_driver/data_model.py:80,93`.\n* **TypedDict used exactly twice** — should either be `BaseModel` (validated) or `dict[str, Any]` (honest).\n\n### Dependency injection vs globals\n\n* `default_prompt_store()` **is** `@cache`**-decorated** at `packages/inference/prompts/store.py:37`. 12+ call sites do `default_prompt_store().load(\"...\")` inline. Hidden global state — tests must monkeypatch the module attribute.\n* **Two** `default_prompt_store` **functions:** the canonical one and a shadow at `services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/prompt_store.py:30` with different render mode and env var. Greppers find either.\n* `_ACTIVE_MODEL_SET: ContextVar` + env var fallback in `tiers.py:42,86,121-146` — two parallel injection channels with buried precedence rules.\n\n**Good pattern that should be applied everywhere:** `WorkerStorageAdapters` (`packages/storage/worker.py:42`) is a `@dataclass(frozen=True)` of repositories passed through `create_app(storage_adapters=...)`. Proper DI. The contrast with `default_prompt_store()` shows the team knows the right pattern.\n\n---\n\n## Security surface (deep audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### SSRF via user-controlled URLs\n\n`services/worker/agents/ResearchDriver/research_agent/retrieval_packs.py` and `http_client.py` fetch URLs derived from search results and user-supplied input. No allowlist, no blocked-IP-range check (RFC 1918, loopback, link-local), no scheme validation. An adversary who controls a search result or question URL can direct the worker to internal services (`http://169.254.169.254/`, `http://localhost:5432/`, etc.).\n\n### Secrets in logs / plaintext config\n\n`ApiConfig` is a plain frozen dataclass. Any `logger.info(f\"Starting with config {config}\")` dumps `ELIJAH_API_KEY`, `LITELLM_MASTER_KEY` in plaintext. No `SecretStr`, no `__repr__` override, no redaction filter on handlers. The structured log setup makes this worse — when JSON logging is eventually wired into the worker, every config-log line will be machine-readable credential exfiltration.\n\n### `extra=\"ignore\"` on LLM response models as a prompt-injection vector\n\nLLM output models silently drop unknown fields (`extra=\"ignore\"`). A jailbroken or compromised LLM returning `{\"action\": \"delete_all\", ...}` passes validation — the extra field is silently discarded. Switching to `extra=\"forbid\"` makes unexpected fields a hard error, surfacing prompt injection attempts rather than silently laundering them.\n\n### No authentication or authorisation model documented\n\nThe FastAPI app has no visible middleware for auth (`services/api/app.py`). No JWT validation, no API key check, no rate limiting. If this is intentional (internal-only service), it is not documented. An investor or auditor will ask; the answer needs to be explicit.\n\n### `subprocess.Popen` without shell=False enforcement\n\n`tools/cli/commands.py:710,779` — `Popen` without `with` and without verifying `shell=False`. If any argument is ever constructed from user input, this becomes command injection. Currently appears safe, but the pattern is fragile.\n\n### Hardcoded user-agent string\n\n`pdf_download.py:111` — `\"Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; ElijahBot/1.0)\"`. Not a security vulnerability per se, but discloses the bot identity and product name to every third-party server the worker touches.\n\n---\n\n## Dependency hygiene (audit checklist, 2026-05-12)\n\n### CVE scan not in CI\n\n`pip-audit` / `uv run pip-audit` is not in the CI pipeline. Known CVEs in dependencies go undetected until manually checked. Run: `uv run pip-audit` and add it to the pre-push checklist.\n\n### Floating version pins\n\n`pyproject.toml` uses `>=` lower bounds for most dependencies. Any `uv lock --upgrade` can silently pull in a breaking minor release or a CVE-carrying patch. Pin transitive dependencies via the lockfile and verify upgrades explicitly. At minimum, review the lockfile diff on every dependency bump PR.\n\n### LiteLLM pinning\n\nLiteLLM releases multiple times per week and has had breaking changes in minor versions. The version constraint should be pinned to a specific minor (e.g., `==1.x.y`) and upgraded deliberately, not opportunistically.\n\n---\n\n## Operational readiness (audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### No metrics endpoint\n\nThe API has no `/metrics` (Prometheus) or equivalent. There is no way to scrape request latency, queue depth, LLM call counts, or error rates. Observability is currently log-only, and — as noted above — worker logs are largely invisible. An investor asking \"how do you monitor production?\" has no answer from the code.\n\n### Single health check — litellm only\n\n`services/api/litellm_check.py` checks LiteLLM connectivity at startup. There is no ongoing `/health` or `/readiness` endpoint that checks DB connectivity, queue connectivity, or object-store reachability. Container orchestrators (k8s, ECS) cannot distinguish \"process alive\" from \"process serving traffic correctly\".\n\n### No alerting story\n\nNo integration with PagerDuty, Opsgenie, or equivalent. No `Sentry` / `Honeybadger` / `Rollbar` error tracking. Errors surface only if someone reads the logs. For a product claiming prediction accuracy, silent failures in the research pipeline are a business risk.\n\n### Worker has no graceful shutdown\n\n`services/worker/queue_runner.py` has a `future.cancel()` that does not actually cancel in-flight work (noted above). There is no SIGTERM handler that drains the current job before exit. Kubernetes pod eviction mid-research-run produces a partially-written graph with no recovery path.\n\n### No structured runbook\n\nNo `docs/runbook.md` or equivalent. An on-call engineer hitting an incident has no documented procedure. For investor due diligence, the absence of operational documentation is a flag.\n\n---\n\n## API contract stability (audit, 2026-05-12)\n\n### No versioned endpoints\n\nAll routes are at `/` or `/api/...` with no version prefix (`/v1/`). Any breaking change to a response schema silently breaks callers. There is no deprecation story. If a third party (demo partner, investor sandbox) integrates against the API, a schema change has no migration path.\n\n### No schema registry\n\nThe Pydantic DTOs in `packages/contracts/` serve as the implicit API schema, but they are not published or versioned. There is no OpenAPI schema pinned in CI (the auto-generated `/openapi.json` changes with every model change). Breaking changes are invisible until a client fails at runtime.\n\n### `data: dict[str, Any]` on `QuestionRead`\n\nThe primary question read DTO carries a freeform `dict[str, Any]` field. This makes the API contract impossible to version or document precisely. Any typed field added inside `data` is invisible to OpenAPI and undetectable by clients without runtime inspection.\n\n### No changelog or deprecation policy\n\nNo `CHANGELOG.md`, no `DEPRECATED` markers on removed fields, no migration guide. This is acceptable for a pre-launch internal API but must be in place before any external integration.\n\n---\n\n## Production runtime telemetry (queried 2026-05-12, Betty / Postgres)\n\nQueried directly from the production Postgres database on Betty (`elijah` DB). All data is from the system's entire operational lifetime.\n\n### Lifetime job volume\n\n| Metric | Value |\n| -- | -- |\n| Total jobs ever run | 6 |\n| Date range | 2026-05-07 only |\n| Completed jobs | 0 |\n| Partial jobs | 1 |\n| Failed jobs | 5 (83%) |\n\nThe system has never produced a completed job. The single successful `run` record was produced by the `partial` job.\n\n### Failure breakdown (all 5 failed jobs)\n\n| Root cause | Count | Notes |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| Missing `question.data.domain` field | 2 | Config/data error; not a code bug |\n| Silent failure — no error recorded | 1 | **Confirms bare** `except` **swallowing in production** |\n| Worker restart orphaned job | 1 | **Confirms graceful-shutdown gap in production** |\n| Worker stuck — no LLM calls reaching LiteLLM proxy | 1 | Connectivity or config issue |\n\n### The one successful run — step timing\n\n| Step | Duration |\n| -- | -- |\n| `indicator_extraction` | 233.7s |\n| `extraction` | 172.7s |\n| `research` | 142.1s |\n| `driver_indicator_literature` | 123.1s |\n| `inference` | 0.5s |\n| `setup` / `priors` / `report` | ~0s each |\n| **Total wall time** | **~11.25 minutes** |\n\n### LLM cost tracking is completely broken\n\n`runs.total_llm_calls`, `runs.total_llm_tokens`, `runs.total_llm_usd` are all NULL on the completed run. `run_steps.llm_calls`, `llm_input_tokens`, `llm_output_tokens`, `llm_usd`, `llm_by_model` are all NULL for every step. The schema exists to capture this; the code never writes to it.\n\nLiteLLM's own spend log (`LiteLLM_SpendLogs`) shows only 1 call for $0.0001 from 2026-04-23 — the research pipeline's LLM calls are entirely absent. **There is currently no way to know what a research run costs.**\n\n### Other instrumentation gaps confirmed\n\n* `runs.git_branch` and `runs.app_version` are NULL — no build provenance on runs\n* `runs.total_seconds` is NULL on the completed run despite `run_steps` having individual durations — the rollup is never computed\n* `job_events` has 41 `progress` events, 6 `claimed`, 3 `failed`, 1 `completed` — event spine exists but is not surfaced anywhere\n\n### Artifact inventory (from the one successful run)\n\n| Kind | Count | Size |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| `graph_objects` | 1 | 332 KB |\n| `audit_summary` | 2 | 71 KB |\n| `drivers` | 2 | 101 KB |\n| `sources` | 2 | 59 KB |\n| `run_summary_json` | 2 | 29 KB |\n| `indicators_graph` | 1 | 29 KB |\n| `qc_index` | 4 | 22 KB |\n| `driver_indicators` | 1 | 23 KB |\n| `report` | 2 | 12 KB |\n| `question_summary` | 1 | 5 KB |\n| `run_summary_text` | 2 | 4 KB |\n| `artifacts_index` | 2 | 7 KB |\n| `other` | 2 | 3 KB |\n\n### Summary assessment\n\nThe production database confirms the three most urgent audit findings are not theoretical — silent exception swallowing and missing graceful shutdown have already caused job failures. LLM cost tracking being entirely unwritten means the team has no data on what the system costs to operate.\n\n---\n\n## Approach\n\nReference `lookout.md` as the authoritative list. Highest-impact items before any investor technical review:\n\n 1. Wire `configure_logging` into the worker entry (`tools/cli/commands.py:489`) — otherwise the structured-log infrastructure is theatre\n 2. **Fix LLM cost tracking** — write to `runs.total_llm_calls/usd` and `run_steps.llm_*` fields; currently all NULL and the system has no cost visibility\n 3. Strip the `Role: / Where It Is Used:` boilerplate from module docstrings\n 4. Define a `GraphLike` Protocol — eliminates `graph: Any` cascade\n 5. Fix sync I/O inside async API route handlers (`documents.py:388-465, 740-807`) — event-loop blocker\n 6. Add `selectinload`/`joinedload` discipline in `sql_repositories.py` — kill the pervasive N+1\n 7. Move `create_all()` out of repository constructors to application startup\n 8. Replace `extra=\"ignore\"` with `extra=\"forbid\"` on LLM output models — silent field drop is a debugging nightmare and a prompt-injection blind spot\n 9. Replace `datetime.utcnow()` with `datetime.now(UTC)`; replace `datetime.now().astimezone()` in `gating_store.py` with `datetime.now(UTC)`\n10. Replace `requests.Session()` instances with context-managed clients\n11. Adopt `pydantic-settings` for `core/settings.py`; consolidate the 35+ scattered `os.environ` reads\n12. Apply `SecretStr` to API keys in `ApiConfig` and related — prevent plaintext key logging\n13. Add SSRF protection to the URL-fetching paths in `retrieval_packs.py` / `http_client.py`\n14. Add SIGTERM graceful-shutdown handler to the worker — already caused a job failure in production\n15. Use the `DomainError` hierarchy in workers and core pipelines, not just the API\n16. Replace `RuntimeError(\"Unreachable: ...\")` with `assert False` or typed internal-error\n17. Replace `str` with `StrEnum`/`Literal` on contract fields (`classification`, `relevance`, `target_type`, etc.)\n18. Replace `\"\"` as `None` sentinel with `str | None = None`\n19. Clean storage mapper defensive wrapping (most visible per-file smell)\n20. Break up `_run_single_step_impl()` and `run_research_driver_for_question()`\n21. Extract config dataclass from 45-parameter `run_research_driver_over_questions()`\n22. Refactor `retrieval_packs.py` copy-pasted classes\n23. Extract base class for the three `fermi_*.py` files\n24. Split `packages/storage/__init__.py` barrel alongside `sql_repositories.py`\n25. Add a centralised retry policy / thread pool configuration; remove magic constants\n26. See SPI-254 for documentation gaps (ADRs, CONVENTIONS.md, runbook, onboarding)\n27. Add `pip-audit` to CI and pin LiteLLM to a specific minor version\n28. Add a `/health` readiness endpoint that checks DB + queue reachability\n29. Add `/v1/` prefix to all API routes and pin the OpenAPI schema in CI\n\n## Tooling\n\n* `uvx vulture packages/ core/ services/ --min-confidence 80` — dead code\n* `uv run ruff check . --select F401` — unused imports\n* `uv run mypy --strict` — would surface most `Any` / `Optional` / missing return type issues (not in CI)\n* `uv run pip-audit` — CVE scan against locked dependencies\n* Add `pytest-asyncio` and tests exercising async lifecycle / cancellation\n* Add `pytest-httpserver` to replace mock-based HTTP tests\n* Add a semgrep rule for `datetime.utcnow()` and `datetime.now()` without timezone\n* Add a semgrep rule for `os.environ` outside `core/settings.py`\n* Add a semgrep rule for `requests.Session()` without context manager"

At scale it will be catastrophic.\n\n### Whole-table scans to build lookups\n\n`sql_repositories.py:1260,1274` — `session.scalars(select(NodeRow)).all()` loads every row in the table to build a dict.\n\n### Delete-all-then-rewrite as the save model\n\n`save_nodes`, `save_edges`, `save_graph_store_bundle` (`sql_repositories.py:1261-1266, 1273-1278, 1384-1416`) issue `delete(...)` against every row then re-add. Pre-fork instantiation → child processes share parent's pooled FDs.
